创建首页（倒计时卡 + 今日概览卡 + 玻璃路线图容器 + 悬浮新建钮 + 轻引导浮层）：


Command: create
Created File with Text:
```
// @title YORO
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import Taro, { useDidShow } from '@tarojs/taro'
import { getMainlines, getMainlineDetail } from '@/db/api'
import type { MainlineDetail } from '@/db/types'
import { countdown, prettyDate, todayStr } from '@/utils/date'
import Roadmap from '@/components/Roadmap'

export default function Home() {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [mainlines, setMainlines] = useState<{ id: string; name: string; exam_date: string }[]>([])
  const [activeId, setActiveId] = useState('')
  const [detail, setDetail] = useState<MainlineDetail | null>(null)
  const [showGuide, setShowGuide] = useState(false)
  const mapVersion = useRef(0)

  const loadAll = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true)
    setError('')
    try {
      const lines = await getMainlines()
      if (lines.length === 0) {
        setMainlines([])
        setDetail(null)
        if (!Taro.getStorageSync('yoro_guide_seen')) setShowGuide(true)
        return
      }
      if (!Taro.getStorageSync('yoro_guide_seen')) setShowGuide(true)
      setMainlines(lines.map((l) => ({ id: l.id, name: l.name, exam_date: l.exam_date })))
      const target = activeId && lines.some((l) => l.id === activeId) ? activeId : lines[0].id
      setActiveId(target)
      const d = await getMainlineDetail(target)
      setDetail(d)
    } catch {
      setError('地图加载失败，请下拉重试')
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }, [activeId])

  useEffect(() => { loadAll() }, [loadAll])
  useDidShow(() => { loadAll() })

  const switchLine = useCallback(async (id: string) => {
    if (id === activeId) return
    setActiveId(id)
    setLoading(true)
    try {
      const d = await getMainlineDetail(id)
      setDetail(d)
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }, [activeId])

  const closeGuide = useCallback(() => {
    Taro.setStorageSync('yoro_guide_seen', '1')
    setShowGuide(false)
  }, [])

  // 今日概览：今日已走格数 + 当前在走节点
  const overview = useMemo(() => {
    if (!detail) return { todayDone: 0, current: null as null | { branch: string; node: string } }
    const today = todayStr()
    let todayDone = 0
    let current: { branch: string; node: string } | null = null
    for (const b of detail.branches) {
      if (b.type !== 'subject' || b.status === 'archived') continue
      const sorted = [...b.nodes].sort((x, y) => x.order_index - y.order_index)
      todayDone += sorted.filter((n) => n.status === 'done' && n.done_date === today).length
      if (!current && b.status !== 'hibernate') {
        const frontier = sorted.find((n) => n.status === 'planned')
        if (frontier) current = { branch: b.name, node: frontier.name }
      }
    }
    return { todayDone, current }
  }, [detail])

  const active = mainlines.find((m) => m.id === activeId)

  if (loading && mainlines.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen paper-texture flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3">
        <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full border-4 border-primary border-t-transparent animate-spin" />
        <p className="text-xl text-muted-foreground">正在铺开地图…</p>
      </div>
    )
  }

  if (error && mainlines.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen paper-texture flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-4 px-8">
        <div className="i-lucide-map-off w-16 h-16 text-muted-foreground" />
        <p className="text-xl text-muted-foreground">{error}</p>
        <button type="button" className="press-sketch rounded-sketch bg-primary px-6 py-3" onClick={() => loadAll()}>
          <div className="py-1 text-xl text-primary-foreground">重新加载</div>
        </button>
      </div>
    )
  }

  // 轻引导浮层（可跳过）
  const guideOverlay = showGuide && (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-foreground/30 flex items-center justify-center px-6">
      <div className="glass-strong rounded-sketch-lg w-full p-5 flex flex-col gap-4 animate-sketch-in">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <p className="text-2xl font-hand text-foreground">开始之前</p>
          <button type="button" className="press-sketch text-base text-muted-foreground" onClick={closeGuide}>跳过</button>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-2xl bg-primary/12 border border-primary/30 flex items-center justify-center flex-none">
            <div className="i-lucide-map-pin w-5 h-5 text-primary" />
          </div>
          <div className="flex-1">
            <p className="text-xl text-foreground">写下终点去哪里</p>
            <p className="text-base text-muted-foreground mt-0.5">先定一个想抵达的考试日，路线就有了方向。</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-2xl bg-accent/20 border border-accent/40 flex items-center justify-center flex-none">
            <div className="i-lucide-route w-5 h-5 text-accent-foreground" />
          </div>
          <div className="flex-1">
            <p className="text-xl text-foreground">看地图怎么走</p>
            <p className="text-base text-muted-foreground mt-0.5">走过的点亮成格，歇几天也没关系，不催你。</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <button type="button" className="press-sketch rounded-sketch bg-primary mt-1" onClick={closeGuide}>
          <div className="py-3 text-xl text-primary-foreground">知道了</div>
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  )

  // 空态：还没建主线
  if (mainlines.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen paper-texture flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-6 px-8 relative">
        <div className="i-lucide-compass w-20 h-20 text-primary" />
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-hand text-foreground text-center">把你的备考画成一条路</h1>
        <p className="text-xl text-muted-foreground text-center leading-relaxed">计划有时刻，记录无时长。先写下你的终点，或看看示例地图长什么样。</p>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 w-full">
          <button type="button" className="press-sketch rounded-sketch bg-primary shadow-sketch w-full" onClick={() => Taro.navigateTo({ url: '/pages/create-mainline/index' })}>
            <div className="py-3 text-xl text-primary-foreground">写下我的终点</div>
          </button>
          <button type="button" className="press-sketch rounded-sketch bg-card/60 border border-primary/50 w-full" onClick={() => Taro.navigateTo({ url: '/pages/templates/index?demo=1' })}>
            <div className="py-3 text-xl text-primary">先随便看看示例地图</div>
          </button>
        </div>
        {guideOverlay}
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen paper-texture flex flex-col relative">
      {guideOverlay}

      <div className="px-4 pt-3 pb-28 flex flex-col gap-4">
        {/* 倒计时卡片 */}
        <div className="glass rounded-sketch-lg p-4">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div className="flex flex-col">
              <p className="text-base text-muted-foreground">距离终点</p>
              <div className="flex items-end gap-1">
                <span className="text-5xl font-hand text-primary leading-none">{active ? countdown(active.exam_date) : '—'}</span>
                <span className="text-xl text-muted-foreground mb-1">天</span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-base text-muted-foreground mt-1">{active?.name} · {active?.exam_date}</p>
            </div>
            <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl bg-primary/10 border border-primary/25 flex items-center justify-center flex-none">
              <div className="i-lucide-flag w-7 h-7 text-primary" />
            </div>
          </div>
          {mainlines.length > 1 && (
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 mt-3">
              {mainlines.map((m) => {
                const on = m.id === activeId
                return (
                  <button
                    key={m.id}
                    type="button"
                    className={`press-sketch rounded-full px-3 py-1 border ${on ? 'bg-primary border-primary' : 'bg-card/60 border-border/60'}`}
                    onClick={() => switchLine(m.id)}
                  >
                    <span className={`text-base ${on ? 'text-primary-foreground' : 'text-foreground'}`}>{m.name}</span>
                  </button>
                )
              })}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* 今日概览卡片 */}
        <div className="glass rounded-sketch-lg p-4 flex flex-col gap-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <div className="i-lucide-footprints w-6 h-6 text-primary flex-none" />
            <span className="text-xl text-foreground">今日已走 {overview.todayDone} 格</span>
          </div>
          {overview.current ? (
            <p className="text-base text-muted-foreground leading-snug">当前在走：{overview.current.branch} · {overview.current.node}</p>
          ) : (
            <p className="text-base text-muted-foreground">今天还没有进行中的节点，从记一笔开始吧</p>
          )}
          <button type="button" className="press-sketch rounded-sketch bg-primary mt-1" onClick={() => Taro.navigateTo({ url: `/pages/record/index?mainline=${activeId}` })}>
            <div className="py-3 text-xl text-primary-foreground flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
              <div className="i-lucide-pencil-line w-5 h-5" />
              <span>今天做成了什么</span>
            </div>
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* 路线图：单个纵向玻璃容器 */}
        {detail ? (
          <Roadmap
            key={`${activeId}-${mapVersion.current}`}
            detail={detail}
            onNodeClick={(nodeId) => Taro.navigateTo({ url: `/pages/node-detail/index?id=${nodeId}` })}
          />
        ) : (
          <div className="glass rounded-sketch-lg flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 py-16">
            <div className="i-lucide-route w-14 h-14 text-muted-foreground" />
            <p className="text-xl text-muted-foreground">这条主线还没有支线，去新建一条吧</p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* 右下悬浮：新建主线 */}
      <button
        type="button"
        className="fixed right-5 bottom-24 w-14 h-14 rounded-full bg-primary shadow-sketch flex items-center justify-center press-sketch z-40"
        onClick={() => Taro.navigateTo({ url: '/pages/create-mainline/index' })}
      >
        <div className="i-lucide-plus w-7 h-7 text-primary-foreground" />
      </button>

      {/* 底部日期 */}
      <p className="fixed left-0 right-0 bottom-24 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground pointer-events-none">{prettyDate(todayStr())}</p>
    </div>
  )
}

```
```
// @title 我的
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import Taro, { useDidShow } from '@tarojs/taro'
import { archiveMainline, getAllBranches, getMainlines } from '@/db/api'
import type { Branch, Mainline } from '@/db/types'
import { countdown, fmt, todayStr } from '@/utils/date'
import { supabase } from '@/client/supabase'

function buildIcs(mainlines: Mainline[], nodes: { name: string; plan_date: string; plan_start: string; plan_end: string }[]): string {
  const dt = (d: string, t?: string) => `${d.replace(/-/g, '')}T${(t ?? '09:00').replace(':', '')}00`
  const lines: string[] = [
    'BEGIN:VCALENDAR',
    'VERSION:2.0',
    'PRODID:-//YORO//Plan Export//CN',
    'CALSCALE:GREGORIAN',
  ]
  nodes.forEach((n, i) => {
    lines.push('BEGIN:VEVENT')
    lines.push(`UID:yoro-node-${i}@yoro`)
    lines.push(`DTSTART:${dt(n.plan_date, n.plan_start)}`)
    lines.push(`DTEND:${dt(n.plan_date, n.plan_end)}`)
    lines.push(`SUMMARY:${n.name}`)
    lines.push('END:VEVENT')
  })
  mainlines.forEach((m, i) => {
    lines.push('BEGIN:VEVENT')
    lines.push(`UID:yoro-exam-${i}@yoro`)
    lines.push(`DTSTART;VALUE=DATE:${m.exam_date.replace(/-/g, '')}`)
    lines.push(`DTEND;VALUE=DATE:${fmt(new Date(new Date(m.exam_date).getTime() + 86400000)).replace(/-/g, '')}`)
    lines.push(`SUMMARY:${m.name} · 考试日`)
    lines.push('END:VEVENT')
  })
  lines.push('END:VCALENDAR')
  return lines.join('\r\n')
}

const ENTRIES = [
  { icon: 'i-lucide-calendar-days', title: '排期日历', desc: '日/周时间网格，拖动调计划', url: '/pages/schedule/index' },
  { icon: 'i-lucide-book-open-text', title: '今日日记 · 回声', desc: '记录心情与做成了什么', url: '/pages/diary/index' },
  { icon: 'i-lucide-footprints', title: '习惯脚印', desc: '每日/每周的小脚印', url: '/pages/habits/index' },
  { icon: 'i-lucide-bar-chart-3', title: '数据看板', desc: '已走段数与脚印，互不换算', url: '/pages/stats/index' },
  { icon: 'i-lucide-library', title: '起点模板库', desc: '按考试类型一键导入', url: '/pages/templates/index' },
  { icon: 'i-lucide-map', title: '示例地图', desc: '看看一条完整的备考路线', url: '/pages/templates/index?demo=1' },
  { icon: 'i-lucide-hourglass', title: '静默计时', desc: '免打扰，时间不进地图', url: '/pages/timer/index' },
]

export default function Profile() {
  const [lines, setLines] = useState<Mainline[]>([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [exporting, setExporting] = useState(false)
  const [nickname, setNickname] = useState('轻登录 · 未授权')

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true)
    try {
      setLines(await getMainlines(true))
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => { load() }, [load])
  useDidShow(() => { load() })

  useEffect(() => {
    supabase.auth.getSession().then(({ data }) => {
      const email = data.session?.user?.email
      if (email) setNickname(`已登录 · ${email.slice(0, 12)}`)
      else setNickname('轻登录 · 未授权')
    }).catch(() => {})
  }, [])

  async function handleArchive(m: Mainline) {
    try {
      await archiveMainline(m.id, !m.archived)
      await load()
      Taro.showToast({ title: m.archived ? '已恢复' : '已归档', icon: 'success' })
    } catch {
      Taro.showToast({ title: '操作失败', icon: 'none' })
    }
  }

  async function handleExport() {
    setExporting(true)
    try {
      const allLines = await getMainlines(true)
      const branches: Branch[] = await getAllBranches()
      const nodeRows: { name: string; plan_date: string; plan_start: string; plan_end: string }[] = []
      for (const b of branches) {
        const { data } = await supabase.from('nodes').select('name, plan_date, plan_start, plan_end').eq('branch_id', b.id).eq('status', 'planned').not('plan_date', 'is', null)
        if (Array.isArray(data)) nodeRows.push(...(data as typeof nodeRows))
      }
      const ics = buildIcs(allLines, nodeRows)
      if (Taro.getEnv() === 'WEAPP') {
        const fs = Taro.getFileSystemManager()
        const path = `${Taro.env.USER_DATA_PATH}/yoro-plan.ics`
        fs.writeFileSync(path, ics, 'utf8')
        Taro.showToast({ title: `已导出 ${nodeRows.length} 个计划`, icon: 'success' })
        Taro.shareFileMessage({ filePath: path, fileName: 'yoro-plan.ics' }).catch(() => {})
      } else {
        const blob = new Blob([ics], { type: 'text/calendar' })
        const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob)
        const a = document.createElement('a')
        a.href = url
        a.download = 'yoro-plan.ics'
        a.click()
        URL.revokeObjectURL(url)
        Taro.showToast({ title: '已导出日历文件', icon: 'success' })
      }
    } catch {
      Taro.showToast({ title: '导出失败，请重试', icon: 'none' })
    } finally {
      setExporting(false)
    }
  }

  const active = lines.filter((l) => !l.archived)
  const archived = lines.filter((l) => l.archived)

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen paper-texture flex flex-col px-4 py-4 gap-4">
      {/* 头部 */}
      <div className="glass rounded-sketch-lg p-4 flex items-center gap-3">
        <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-full bg-primary/12 border border-primary/30 flex items-center justify-center">
          <div className="i-lucide-user w-8 h-8 text-primary" />
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-0.5">
          <p className="text-xl text-foreground font-hand">{nickname}</p>
          <p className="text-base text-muted-foreground">所有内容仅你可见</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 主线列表 */}
      <div className="glass rounded-sketch-lg p-4 flex flex-col gap-3">
        <p className="text-xl text-foreground font-hand">我的主线</p>
        {loading && <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full border-4 border-primary border-t-transparent animate-spin self-center" />}
        {!loading && active.length === 0 && <p className="text-xl text-muted-foreground">还没有主线，去新建一条</p>}
        {active.map((m) => (
          <div key={m.id} className="rounded-sketch bg-card/60 border border-border/50 p-3 flex items-center justify-between">
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-0.5 min-w-0">
              <span className="text-lg text-foreground truncate">{m.name}</span>
              <span className="text-base text-muted-foreground">考试日 {m.exam_date} · {countdown(m.exam_date)} 天</span>
            </div>
            <button type="button" className="press-sketch rounded-sketch-sm bg-muted border border-border/50 px-3 py-1 flex-none" onClick={() => handleArchive(m)}>
              <span className="text-base text-muted-foreground">归档</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        ))}
        {archived.length > 0 && (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            <p className="text-base text-muted-foreground">已归档（仍可回看）</p>
            {archived.map((m) => (
              <div key={m.id} className="rounded-sketch bg-muted/60 border border-dashed border-border/60 p-3 flex items-center justify-between">
                <div className="flex flex-col gap-0.5 min-w-0">
                  <span className="text-lg text-muted-foreground truncate">{m.name}</span>
                  <span className="text-base text-muted-foreground">考试日 {m.exam_date}</span>
                </div>
                <button type="button" className="press-sketch rounded-sketch-sm bg-card border border-border/50 px-3 py-1 flex-none" onClick={() => handleArchive(m)}>
                  <span className="text-base text-foreground">恢复</span>
                </button>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* 次级入口 */}
      <div className="glass rounded-sketch-lg p-2 flex flex-col">
        {ENTRIES.map((item) => (
          <button
            key={item.title}
            type="button"
            className="press-sketch rounded-sketch-sm flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-3"
            onClick={() => Taro.navigateTo({ url: item.url })}
          >
            <div className={`${item.icon} w-7 h-7 text-primary flex-none`} />
            <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col items-start gap-0.5">
              <span className="text-xl text-foreground">{item.title}</span>
              <span className="text-base text-muted-foreground">{item.desc}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="i-lucide-chevron-right w-5 h-5 text-muted-foreground flex-none" />
          </button>
        ))}
        {/* 导出到日历 */}
        <button type="button" className="press-sketch rounded-sketch-sm flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-3" onClick={handleExport}>
          <div className="i-lucide-calendar-down w-7 h-7 text-primary flex-none" />
          <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col items-start gap-0.5">
            <span className="text-xl text-foreground">导出到日历</span>
            <span className="text-base text-muted-foreground">计划带起止时刻，考试日为全天</span>
          </div>
          <div className="i-lucide-chevron-right w-5 h-5 text-muted-foreground flex-none" />
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* 皮肤与版本 */}
      <div className="glass rounded-sketch-lg p-4 flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <div className="w-6 h-6 rounded-md bg-primary/80 border border-primary" />
          <span className="text-lg text-foreground">当前皮肤：浅天蓝 · 玻璃</span>
        </div>
        <span className="text-base text-muted-foreground">{fmt(todayStr())}</span>
      </div>

      {exporting && (

            <span className="text-lg text-foreground">正在生成日历文件…</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}
```

```
// @title 排期日历
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import Taro, { useDidShow } from '@tarojs/taro'
import { getMainlines } from '@/db/api'
import ScheduleView from '@/components/ScheduleView'

export default function Schedule() {
  const [mainlines, setMainlines] = useState<{ id: string; name: string }[]>([])
  const [activeId, setActiveId] = useState('')
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true)
    try {
      const lines = await getMainlines()
      setMainlines(lines.map((l) => ({ id: l.id, name: l.name })))
      setActiveId((prev) => (prev && lines.some((l) => l.id === prev) ? prev : lines[0]?.id ?? ''))
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => { load() }, [load])
  useDidShow(() => { load() })

  const active = useMemo(() => mainlines.find((m) => m.id === activeId), [mainlines, activeId])

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen paper-texture flex flex-col">
      {/* 顶部说明 + 主线切换 */}
      <div className="px-4 pt-3 pb-1 flex flex-col gap-2">
        <div className="glass rounded-sketch-lg px-4 py-2">
          <p className="text-base text-muted-foreground">这里排的是打算什么时候走；走完了回地图记一笔，不看用了多久。</p>
        </div>
        {mainlines.length > 1 && (
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            {mainlines.map((m) => {
              const on = m.id === activeId
              return (
                <button
                  key={m.id}
                  type="button"
                  className={`press-sketch rounded-full px-3 py-1 border ${on ? 'bg-primary border-primary' : 'bg-card/60 border-border/60'}`}
                  onClick={() => setActiveId(m.id)}
                >
                  <span className={`text-base ${on ? 'text-primary-foreground' : 'text-foreground'}`}>{m.name}</span>
                </button>
              )
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {loading ? (
        <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 py-20">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full border-4 border-primary border-t-transparent animate-spin" />
          <p className="text-xl text-muted-foreground">正在展开排期…</p>
        </div>
      ) : active ? (
        <ScheduleView mainlineId={activeId} />
      ) : (
        <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 py-20 px-8">
          <div className="i-lucide-calendar-off w-14 h-14 text-muted-foreground" />
          <p className="text-xl text-muted-foreground">还没有主线，先去新建一条吧</p>
          <button type="button" className="press-sketch rounded-sketch bg-primary px-6 py-2" onClick={() => Taro.navigateTo({ url: '/pages/create-mainline/index' })}>
            <div className="py-1 text-xl text-primary-foreground">新建主线</div>
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}
```

```
  const [importing, setImporting] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [demoDetail, setDemoDetail] = useState<MainlineDetail | null>(null)
  const [demoLoading, setDemoLoading] = useState(true)
  const isDemo = useMemo(() => {
    const params = Taro.getCurrentInstance().router?.params || {}
    return params.demo === '1'
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isDemo) return
    let alive = true
    getMainlines()
      .then((lines) => {
        const first = lines[0]
        if (!first) {
          if (alive) setDemoLoading(false)
          return
        }
        return getMainlineDetail(first.id).then((d) => {
          if (alive) {
            setDemoDetail(d)
            setDemoLoading(false)
          }
        })
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (alive) setDemoLoading(false)
      })
    return () => {
      alive = false
    }
  }, [isDemo])

  function backToMine() {
    Taro.navigateBack().catch(() => Taro.switchTab({ url: '/pages/home/index' }))
  }
```
